## Operation status handover / archival rule

**While an operation is active**, `PROJECT-STATUS.md` displays the full discovered-source/serializer register for that operation, with each newly found source path logged **before** implementation and test updates recorded as they occur. New discoveries are allowed and must appear with a precise status immediately.

**When O06 is truly ACCEPTED** (all G1–G6 accepted with source-backed evidence and DEV/PRD verification): preserve the complete O06 source register, status history, test journals and logs as permanent repository records. Before removing the expanded O06 section from this page, archive its final Markdown snapshot under `catalog/compliance/archive/UE1-O06-SERIALIZER-REGISTER.md` and retain the canonical JSON `UE1-O06-SERIALIZER-REGISTER.json` without deleting records. Replace the entire O06 live register on this page with **one compact O06 ACCEPTED row**, including final commit, acceptance date and links to the archived source inventory and verification evidence. **Never mark O06 complete or hide unresolved statuses before acceptance.** Disable O06's automatic injection into this page once archived; further O06 regression results belong in its archival/evidence files unless they cause reopening.

Then reserve the live detail area for **O07**, using a separate O07 register and new discoveries, with the same update-before-work/test-by-test discipline. Repeat for subsequent operations. This page remains an active-operation dashboard; the detailed source/test archive remains accessible in Git.

## O06 live source serializer register

**Canonical recovery reference:** this table is updated during discovery, mapping, implementation, tests and deployment, not retrospectively.

Found source entries: **294** (R82=26, R83=26, R96=26, U86=49, U97=49, D97=7, C98=50, C227=5, T99=56).

**Current per-entry status:** EXCLUDED=38, MAPPED=256.

**States:** `DISCOVERED` = located in Epic source only; `MAPPED` = source behavior/reader mapped; `PARTIAL` = a subset of the source-defined serializer is implemented/tested, but remaining source-branch verification or code fixes are unresolved; `GAP` = confirmed missing implementation; `BLOCKED` = named prerequisite; `EXCLUDED` = proven non-saveable or runtime-only; `DEV_PASS`/`PRD_PASS` = explicit source-scoped named tests passed at that target; `ACCEPTED` = source contract signed off. An entry that has passed a narrow fixture but may have unresolved fields remains PARTIAL.

**Mandatory live workflow:** (1) On discovering a symbol, add it immediately as DISCOVERED with source location. (2) Update to MAPPED/PARTIAL/GAP when the reader/code is inspected, naming remaining work. (3) Record test script, evidence and exact outcome when run; change the same row before starting another task. (4) Commit/push/deploy the updated status with each bounded checkpoint. (5) Never turn a registration or a corpus-only result into serializer acceptance. A latest DEV/PRD PASS means only that the named fixture passed there; it NEVER means class acceptance while the status is PARTIAL. Check the Outstanding / evidence column for the precise unfinished work. Some PARTIAL entries need only verification; others still need code. When the complete source contract is demonstrated, promote that row explicitly to ACCEPTED with evidence. Never replace an older failure without retaining its journal. The O06 test runner records STARTED then PASS/FAIL here automatically, while leaving class-level acceptance unchanged. The six O06 gates remain fixed even if new rows are discovered.

Machine-readable file: `catalog/compliance/UE1-O06-SERIALIZER-REGISTER.json`. Regenerate/update using `python catalog/bin/sync-ue1-o06-serializers.py` or `--set-status ID STATUS --reader ... --test ... --evidence ... --remaining ...`. Existing statuses survive rescans. Paths below are relative to their corresponding Epic source roots. **These rows are source sightings, not all distinct on-disk requirements.**

### O06 source-version expansion using local Graphify (2026-10-22)

**Discovered and registered immediately:** after indexing other UE1 versions, the live O06 register was extended with **106 source-version sightings**: v0.82 26 registrations (`R82`), v0.83 26 (`R83`), v0.86x 49 (`U86`), and v1.227 5 explicit `Serialize` definitions (`C227`). All 106 entries were recorded first as **DISCOVERED** with local Epic source path/line; 52 early-v9 sightings (R82/R83) were subsequently advanced to **MAPPED** after a reproducible byte-identity proof across 200/200 matching C/C++ source and header files (`tools/graphify/Compare-1996-Versions.py`, [evidence](evidence/o06/20261022-ue1-v9-v082-v083-source-identity.txt)). Both trees are the exact same code for the examined 200 files, so do not treat those sightings as 52 separate code implementation gaps. Their v9 UnrealDB field/reader parity still requires G1 reconciliation. The remaining 54 newer source sightings retain **DISCOVERED** status. Original 172 entries and recorded tests were preserved. The new rows are version-specific comparison work for G1/G2/G3/G4.

**Version facts checked against source:** v0.82 and v0.83 `RES_FILE_VERSION=0x0009`, v0.84a `0x000A`; v0.86x and v0.867 `UNREAL_FILE_VERSION=23`. Equal numeric versions do **not** prove that every serialized subclass layout stayed identical: compare actual native methods, headers and changed structures before mapping the new rows. The v1.227 source tree's provenance is not yet established, so `C227` sightings are discovery-only. No O06 completion gate was added, closed or falsely promoted.

### O06 fixed closure board — source census and remaining gates (2026-10-22)

**Purpose:** Stop open-ended serializer-by-serializer work. This board is the authoritative O06 progress tracker. Older narrative paragraphs describe historical checkpoints, **not** today's remaining-work list. O06 cannot be called complete merely because the currently installed texture/sound/font corpus passes.

**Observed source census (DEV local Epic C++):** v0.84a registers **26** `AUTOREGISTER_RESOURCE` classes; `UCamera`, `UPlayer`, editor `UTransBuffer` are confirmed non-saveable, leaving **23 source-saveable registrations** to reconcile. v0.867 registers **49 unique C++ object classes**; most share inherited `SerializeHeader`/`StandardSerializeData` and some are transient, so 49 is *not* 49 separate on-disk serializers. UT99 v400 source has **49 explicit `::Serialize(FArchive&)` C++ definitions** (including runtime/editor/inherited behaviours); each must be classified, but not every one is a valid disk requirement.

**Important coverage limitation:** `CatalogUE1ClassicNativeObjectReader` handles 11 explicit native object classes and now dispatches 13 source-typed UProperty subclasses through `CatalogUE1ClassicPropertyNativeReader`; every new C98/T99 class/field status is PARTIAL until its entire source contract is proven, returning `class_specific_serializer_unimplemented` for other class-native tails. `CatalogUE1ClassicTaggedPropertyReader` now dispatches `classIndex=0` to `CatalogUE1ClassicClassMetadataReader`: source-typed no-script/empty-default metadata is decoded, while nonempty script/defaults and StateFrame remain explicitly unimplemented. The 92/92 and 2,465/2,465 results are genuine but cover a narrow pinned corpus; they **do not** establish full UE1 class/payload support. This is a confirmed implementation gap, not an audit/documentation problem.

### O06 corrective source provenance and nested UClass fields (2026-10-23)

**Architectural correction:** Unreal v1.227 is NOT UT99. Both can use archive version 69, but game identity cannot be inferred from that number. Previous CatalogUE1ClassicSourceFamily automatically chose UT99 for package versions 62–69; this was wrong and has been removed. The new selector requires an explicit UT99 loader source for otherwise unidentified later UE1 archives; it automatically recognizes Unreal v1.227 only from the pinned version 69 / licensee 227 signature, or from explicit v1.227 loader context for compatible older packages. Top-level class, tagged property and native readers select the same profile and pass it into nested class defaults, inherited fields, arrays, structs and scripts. Unresolved lineage returns an error instead of speculative UT99 data. Unreal v1.227's exact UnrealScript Emitter package class identities are XBeamEmitter, XParticleEmitter and XRopeDeco (native C++ classes carry an A prefix); the game overlay now uses source package ClassIndex identity, not the wrong C++-prefixed archive names. The common UE1 tagged UObject serialization runs first. The available v1.227 Core/Src implementation is incomplete and its unverified core changes are NOT silently replaced by UT99 source rules.

**Additional UClass implementation:** CatalogUE1ClassicStructArrayValueReader now recursively follows source-defined UStructProperty::SerializeItem -> UStruct::SerializeBin for nested struct member descriptors, preserving exact linker references and ArrayDim order. Cyclic/unresolved layouts remain explicit, and a struct with no persistent fields correctly consumes zero bytes. These improvements are shared by class default structs and array inner structs. **Mapped 252 / PARTIAL 2 / EXCLUDED 38; the two UClass source-contract entries remain genuinely open** for unsupported item types and source-incomplete runtime value semantics. Functional/native verification and PRD deployment deferred.

Cross-generation architectural contract: specs/engine-game-source-precedence.md (also linked in AGENTS.md). It is required for UE2/2.5, UE3, UE4 and UE5 going forward, not a claim that those readers have been fully changed now.

UnrealDB previously skipped modern glyph records without decoding their values, did not bound the page's UObject reference, and did not apply the source count invariant. | **IMPLEMENTED — VERIFICATION DEFERRED:** `CatalogUE1ClassicNativeObjectReader` now validates each page texture import/export index, reads every 16-byte source glyph record in bounded batches, retains typed coordinate previews and the SHA-256 of the entire persisted glyph array, and applies Epic's exact 32-bit `CharactersPerPage & (CharactersPerPage-1)` invariant while preserving the original DWORD. Does not perform font rasterization or engine lazy-loading. Existing `T99:UFont::Serialize` only; no test contract or PRD deployment. |

| **G4 — legacy FReachSpec archive v35–53** | Epic Unreal v1.200 `Engine/Inc/UnReach.h::FReachSpec::operator<<` archives `FLOAT fCollisionRadius` and `FLOAT fCollisionHeight` when `Ar.Ver()<54`, then **assigns each to a runtime INT**. The `ULevel` native reader previously returned the archived FLOAT as though it were the loaded collision dimension. | **IMPLEMENTED — VERIFICATION DEFERRED:** `CatalogUE1ClassicLevelNativeReader` now preserves each original archived float separately and exposes the loaded signed INT32 obtained by source's truncation-toward-zero when representable; invalid/overflow C++ conversions remain explicitly indeterminate rather than invented. No extra legacy `bPruned` byte is consumed. |
| **G3 — legacy UProperty flag load v35–61** | Epic Unreal v1.200 `Core/Src/UnProp.cpp::UProperty::Serialize` preserves serialized `PropertyFlags`, whereas UT99 v400 clears bits `0x00080040` **only on loading files <=61**. The shared production reader previously applied that later compatibility cleanup to Unreal v1.200 data too. | **IMPLEMENTED — VERIFICATION DEFERRED:** `CatalogUE1ClassicPropertyNativeReader` now retains Epic original `effective_property_flags` for the pinned v1.200 family, and separately exposes `effective_property_flags_if_ut99_v400_load` and whether the source branches actually disagree. Existing class-default/struct consumers therefore no longer silently use the wrong original-engine flags. |

| **G4 — UT99 v400 UMesh::Serialize triangle/vertex invariant** | Epic UT99 v400 `Engine/Src/UnMesh.cpp::UMesh::Serialize` performs `if( Tris.Num() ) check(Tris(0).iVertex[0]<Verts.Num())` immediately after deserializing `Tris`. The production `UMesh`/`ULodMesh` reader previously retained the correct wire values but did not enforce the engine's source-defined first-triangle reference validity. | **IMPLEMENTED — VERIFICATION DEFERRED:** `CatalogUE1ClassicMeshNativeReader` now rejects nonempty UT99 v62–69 `Tris` when its first triangle's first vertex is outside the serialized `Verts.Num()`; version <=61 follows Unreal v1.200, whose `UMesh::Serialize` has no such assertion. Source-specific check and provenance are reported; no new test contract or acceptance promotion. |

| **G4 — Unreal v1.200 / UT99 v400 UFireTexture::Serialize spark records** | Both pinned Epic `Fire/Src/FractalPrivate.h::FSpark::operator<<` branches archive eight `BYTE` fields (`Type`, `Heat`, `X`, `Y`, `ByteA`, `ByteB`, `ByteC`, `ByteD`) for each element in `UFireTexture::Sparks`. UnrealDB previously skipped the array as an opaque `count * 8` byte range, leaving serialized spark values uninterpreted. | **IMPLEMENTED — VERIFICATION DEFERRED:** `CatalogUE1ClassicNativeObjectReader` now consumes every spark in exact source order, retains typed record previews with a complete native archive SHA-256, keeps the previous count/offset/size fields, and preserves source bounds. Does not execute fire effects or runtime spark expansion; existing `C98:UFireTexture::Serialize` and `T99:UFireTexture::Serialize` entries only. |

| **G4 — C98/T99 UPalette::Serialize** | Unreal v1.200 keeps original archive alpha, but UT99 v400 resets palette alpha to 255 on loading package versions below 66. | **IMPLEMENTED — VERIFICATION DEFERRED:** source-selected alpha load handling now preserves v1.200 alpha and applies the UT99 cleanup only to its own family; returns the alternate UT99-old-loader result, and typed per-color previews without changing source bytes. |
| **G3 — C98/T99 UFunction::Serialize** | UT99 v400 resets legacy parameter count, size and return offset before recomputing from linked child properties, whereas v1.200 retains the archived ABI fields. | **IMPLEMENTED — VERIFICATION DEFERRED:** retains wire ABI and exposes source-selected loaded/recomputation state, without inventing unresolved runtime offsets. |
| **G4 — C98/T99 UModel::Serialize FLightMapIndex** | Unreal v1.200 pre-v53 uses version-gated light-map fields; UT99 v400 reads its modern FLightMapIndex grammar even from older packages. | **IMPLEMENTED — VERIFICATION DEFERRED:** tries bounded source-defined legacy/modern wire variants alongside already-known sphere variants, selects uniquely complete archive layouts, and preserves honest ambiguity. Empty LightMap arrays are recognized as wire-equivalent. |
| **G4 — T99 ULodMesh::Serialize remap** | UT99 v400 reads `RemapAnimVerts(v)` for each `v < FrameVerts` when the remap list is nonempty. | **IMPLEMENTED — VERIFICATION DEFERRED:** checks the source's required remap-array index range before reporting a decoded record; actual vertex permutation remains runtime-only. |

| **G3/G4 — canonical UE1 engine vs UT99 game-source override (2026-10-23)** | Epic engine baseline L:/Source/Engine/UE1/Source archives FSphere as 16 bytes, with pre-v41 primitive FPlane. Later UT99 v400 has a vector-only pre-v62 FSphere and different old UModel flags and light-map wire fields. Other source differences involve UProperty flags, UFunction loaded ABI, UPalette alpha, FPoly/BSP flags and script bytecode. | **IMPLEMENTED — VERIFICATION DEFERRED:** A shared source-family selector defaults to canonical Epic engine for old versions, UT99 v400 for versions >=62; callers can explicitly select UT99 when loading older game files. Primitive, Model, Mesh, native class, properties, function/class/script, polygon and BSP readers now use the selected pinned-source C++ operator instead of trying guessed source layouts and returning ambiguous status. Version alone is not treated as proof of older package provenance. No tests or PRD deploy. |

| **G3 — source-family propagation through nested class/default/property decoding (batch 2026-10-23)** | Last source-hierarchy batch selected an Epic or game-specific serializer only at the direct native reader. Nested UClass metadata, imported ancestor property definitions, UArray/UStruct property fields, class defaults and script substreams still independently defaulted to the baseline from the package version. Epic v1.200 UClass also archives two trailing names in v35-56 that UT99 v400 UClass does not. UT99 v400 handles old fixed StringProperty tags upgraded into dynamic StrProperty irrespective of the package's old version. | **IMPLEMENTED — VERIFICATION DEFERRED:** Source-family choice is now propagated consistently through the object/tagged/class-default/array/struct hierarchy, including imported superclass resolution and source-scoped field caching; version-only legacy String/Array/Map behaviour is now source-scoped. UClass trailer's original-engine gate no longer misreads old packages explicitly loaded by UT99 v400. The CLI inspector accepts --ue1-source=epic-ue1 or --ue1-source=ut99-v400 and reports selected source in JSON/text. Package version still does not invent game provenance; callers explicitly pass it when known. Code-only work, no new tests or acceptance promotion. |

| **G2 — May/July original Unrealfile source routing (2026-10-23 batch)** | The May v0.86x and July v0.867 Epic sources both use package version 23 but differ in CPF_Intrinsic property treatment, FRotation archive width, legacy native headers, script and BSP variants. Production had source-specific readers already, but the common CLI was calling them without passing source lineage and could discard the user's known revision. UBounds (May FBoundingRect, July FBoundingBox) remained permanently labelled as unverified. | **IMPLEMENTED — VERIFICATION DEFERRED:** CLI now accepts --ue1-original-1997=unreal_1997_may or unreal_1997_july and propagates it to original script, BSP, UStackTree, class-property, UProperties, UDatabase, level, model, mesh, texture and related native readers. The shared UDatabase record reader now identifies the correct Epic May/July bounding type and exact FVector Min/Max plus BYTE IsValid archive fields. Existing U86/U97 source entries annotated as affected, not promoted to MAPPED. No functional tests or PRD deployment. |

These changes are tracked against **existing** G2/G3/G4 items only. They remain open until post-implementation code review and verification; G1 remains the only accepted O06 gate. **Do not substitute native Sound/Music exporter counts for G2/G3/G4 completion.**

No O07 bulk decoding or O10 runtime claims.

Combined affected verification **DEV 4/4, PRD 4/4 named tests PASS** with 92 genuine package corpus unchanged. C98/T99 `UEnum::Serialize` advanced from GAP to PARTIAL, not ACCEPTED; full class/parent/version/source census remains open.

**G3 `UConst` progress (2026-10-22):** Implemented Epic v1.200/UT99 v400 `UConst::Serialize` (inherited UField SuperField/Next references followed by source-gated ANSI or UTF-16LE `FString Value`), with strict bounds, terminating-NUL enforcement, invalid reference/count/UTF16 rejection and opaque trailing-extension identification. It traverses Epic `UStruct.Children`, `UField.Next` and local `UClass.SuperField` references, using the same production `CatalogUE1ClassicPropertyNativeReader` to obtain serialized UProperty kind and ArrayDim. A known mismatched type or out-of-range array index throws rather than producing misleading metadata. On genuine pinned v61 compiled .u packages, **1,689 of 5,344 defaults match declarations** (861 direct, 828 from resolvable local superclass exports); 70 classes have every tag's declaration locally matched. Zero conflicts. The other **3,655** refer to imported superclass schemas not contained in the current package; remain unverified and explicitly counted, and all 513 UClass statuses **remain `class_default_tags_structurally_decoded_schema_unverified`** because actual value payload semantics/other inherited property offsets have NOT been proven. No imported dependency is guessed or silently reinterpreted. New source-anchored genuine `verify-ue1-o06-genuine-class-default-declarations.php` **1,042/1,042 DEV PASS** (includes mutated negative type + array-index rejection), with SHA-pinned package identities. Five affected DEV regression scripts **5/5 PASS**, incl. 16 genuine package corpus. The next executable gap is explicit cross-package imported superclass property resolution against source-verified Core/Engine script packages, then robust opaque value decoding. No O06 acceptance or native engine oracle claimed; DEV-only tests and SHA-verified PRD deployment.

**O06 UE1 v61 imported superclass UProperty declarations resolved (2026-10-23):** Prior genuine corpus 513 UClass/default exports had 5,344 tags of which 1,689 were locally verifiable and 3,655 lacked same-package property declarations. Production `CatalogUE1ClassicClassDefaultSchemaReader.php` now follows the **actual `UClass` SuperField negative import reference** through its imported `Class` and **outer imported root `Package`**, loads only that named sibling `.u` package (validated file and package header), locates an **exactly unique exported UClass**, and reads its real `UStruct.Children`/`UField.Next`/`UProperty` declarations. It recursively traverses local and imported class ancestors, guards against cross-file cycles and excessive depth, rejects conflicting known tag type/ArrayDim, and never searches globally for an arbitrary same-named class. It correctly returns **unverified** when the imported parent is absent, invalid or ambiguous. **O06 classic FPropertyTag stream termination correction (2026-10-23):** The shared production `CatalogUE1ClassicTaggedPropertyReader::parseTags` previously invented `MAX_TAGS=20000` and would reject a syntactically valid source tag archive with more than 20,000 tags. **O06 T99 FString source-counted ANSI/Unicode archive decoding (2026-10-23):** Genuine pinned UT99 v400 `Core/Src/UnProp.cpp::UStrProperty::SerializeItem` archives `FString`, and actual `Core/Src/UnMisc.cpp::operator<<(FArchive&,FString&)` persists signed **compact `SaveNum`**. Positive count is the number of serialized ANSI single bytes, negative count its negated 16-bit Unicode code-unit count. This includes terminal NUL; `SaveNum==0` is empty and load-time `A.Num()==1` empties a lone terminator. The prior shared `CatalogUE1ClassicTaggedPropertyReader` returned `StrProperty` tagged values opaque even for perfectly valid UT99 archives, and class-schema matching could misleadingly report a declaration match without distinguishing a malformed dynamic-string value. Original v9/v10 global ResArray lookup keys; original v22/v23 serialized ResMap/declared filenames; classic v34–69 source VerifyImport including class/outer/privacy, V4 SQL + V5 provider matching. Derived UTexture and original primitive database records added; other registered resource classes plus inherited/external binary serializers remain OPEN. No acceptance claim. |

These TEN categories are fixed for UE1; new findings go under an existing operation as defects rather than inventing endless new operation IDs. Acceptance covers each explicitly bounded responsibility, NOT engine-wide parity. Runtime object-loading and actual workflow verification remain O10; property serialization O06 and bulk data O07.

## Mandatory source-first execution and acceptance discipline (2026-10-21)

**Policy correction after repeated source-coverage misses and O01 scope expansion:**

1. **Inventory source before interpreting or editing a format.** For each engine, recursively enumerate ALL supplied engine/game source roots and relevant C++ load/save code; search format tags, headers AND EOF trailers, version gates, serializers, loader functions, and all source revisions. Register the exact source file, symbol/line and branch difference. The absent `UnObjVer.h` is NOT evidence of absent source: original Unreal v0.82–0.84a use `UnRes.cpp` and trailer metadata, not the later `ULinker`.
2. **No unsupported "source gap" assumptions.** Before calling information unavailable, search alternative file names, legacy subsystems, adjacent versions, engine and game trees, actual loader/writer implementations, and compiled artifacts where useful; record searches and distinguish (a) source genuinely absent for a particular fork, (b) UnrealDB reader not yet built, and (c) independent test fixture not yet available. Treat source definitions as implementation authority; do not delay source-driven work pending an optional old executable.
3. **Freeze a narrow operation contract BEFORE coding.** O01 is file-format identification, version/licensee extraction, selected-game dispatch and honest unsupported/invalid results. Full name/import/export decoding is O02–O04; dependencies O05; payloads O06/O07; production SQL/search O09; complete workflow acceptance O10. Later operation failures must not block earlier acceptance unless they actually change its inputs or outputs.
4. **Work each operation in one bounded source→diff→fix→accept sequence:** exhaustive source mapping once, real production caller coverage, minimal correction, source-anchored positive/negative tests, standard regressions, evidence of observed differences, Git commit/push and immediate PRD SHA-256 deployment. Never add arbitrary B-number cycles or new acceptance conditions after work begins without a demonstrated scope error.
5. **Publish measurable results:** per-operation PASS/FAIL and exact open acceptance checks; genuine corpus and regression counts separately; source findings with true status (source present, code absent, or test missing). Do not conflate tests passed with engine accepted. After closing O01, **advance to O02** without reopening O01 for unrelated improvements.

The source-first rule is mandatory for UE1 and repeats unchanged for UE2/2.5, UE3/UDK, UE4 and UE5. Review all supplied code branches before stating that any engine information is missing.

| Source tree | Mechanically observed file version / minimum | Distinction and remaining work |
|---|---|---|
| `Unreal [v0.82]` | `RES_FILE_VERSION=0x0009` | Original EOF resource trailer, source-defined names/imports/exports/native headers and unverified staging implemented. **Type-specific object payloads O06 still open** |
| `Unreal [v0.83]` | `RES_FILE_VERSION=0x0009` | Same source-defined v9 family structurally read and staged; true loaded ResArray runtime binding unavailable, recorded as not-evaluated; payloads O06 |
| `Unreal [v0.84a]` | `RES_FILE_VERSION=0x000A` | Original v10 structural tables/headers read and staged; type/name source lookup projected, payloads O06 |
| `Unreal [v0.86x]` | `UNREAL_FILE_VERSION=23`, min 22 | Early 32-byte `Unrealfile\x1A` header; separate format |
| `Unreal [v0.867]` | Version 23, min 22 | `FUnrealfileSummary`, not modern UE1 package summary |
| `Unreal [v1.200]` | `PACKAGE_FILE_VERSION=61`, min 34 | Classic numeric tag; different pre-v68 summary serializer; original complete source |

| `Unreal [v1.227]` | Version 69, min 60 | Source includes licencee/Legend changes; header/linker bodies incomplete; git HEAD previously `2bd1ce9` |

All paths above are under `L:\Source\Games\Unreal`, `L:\Source\Games\UT99`, or `L:\Source\Engine\UE1`. Each relevant fork must be traced under its correct file-format family. UE2 and newer game roots are reserved for later phases.

**Source coverage clarification (2026-10-21):** Source-code absence is **not** a blocker for the early v0.82/0.83/0.84a resource formats or v0.86x/v0.867 Unrealfile format. Their structural/native metadata readers and typed unverified staging are now implemented, but complete object/property/payload readers remain O06 and onward. The v0.82/0.83 resource versions are **9** and v0.84a is **10**, distinct from later classic package versions 35–69. Unreal v1.224 directory genuinely contains mostly headers (8 C++ files observed); v1.227 contains the file summary/layout headers, while some specific linker implementations are absent. Nearby complete source branches can inform shared behaviour but must not automatically be treated as identical. Genuine old-format binary fixtures and native runtime runs improve *independent validation*; **neither is a prerequisite to read/implement the available documented source**. The remaining production MySQL/search work is an **UnrealDB implementation test**, not an Epic-source gap. See `catalog/compliance/UE1-IMPLEMENTATION.md`.

**Genuine legacy corpus discovered 2026-10-21:** `L:\Source\Engine\UE1\Source\Unreal\Textures` contains 61 standard-magic `.utx` packages: 59 are versions 35–60, and two are version 61; all licensee versions are zero. The other 85 files are PCX images. Per-file SHA-256/version inventory at `catalog/compliance/inventory/ue1-textures-v35-v61.csv`, explanation at `catalog/compliance/inventory/UE1-TEXTURES-CORPUS.md`. **B04 VERIFIED (limited):** all 61/61 SHA-verified files parse via the canonical UE1 reader without reported issues, including matching name/import/export counts and actual production header helper; an independent direct-byte source-defined 44-byte summary read matches all 11 serialized header fields in 61/61 files, and file-examine GUIDs agree in 61/61. These files all have heritageCount=1, heritageOffset=44; they do **not** cover other heritage arrangements. Original compiled Epic engine/runtime comparison and full workflow acceptance remain OPEN.

**B05 verified (bounded):** new installed genuine UE1 v68/v69 corpus 31/31; aggregate genuine-file profile/reader/source-specific V5 container write/read 92/92, including version69/licensee227. The UE1 preliminary classifier no longer admits the UE2-only Killing Floor tag (UE1-D007); UE2-only tag remains admitted under UE2. Two negative controls passed, 19/19 selected regression scripts passed on DEV, and six relevant executable checks passed on deployed PRD. Actual production MySQL publishing and search, early string-tagged binary reader, missing source fork coverage and native Epic executable parity remain **OPEN**.

B02 checks passed: `verify-ue1-early-format-identification.php` **9/9**, plus **12/12** selected UE1/UE2/UE4/UE5 regression scripts, PHP syntax and source-anchored tests. B03 checked the live header-only helper: 4/4 assertions passed after three baseline failures, plus **15/15** selected regressions. **G2 — May/July 1997 per-object and UProperties FRotation discrepancy FIXED (2026-10-23):** Epic v0.86x `UnMath.h:545` archives FRotation as three WORDs (6B, zero-extended to INT); v0.867 `UnMath.h:567` archives three signed INTs (12B), even though both v22/v23 envelope versions overlap. The existing production `CatalogUE1OriginalUnrealfileBinaryPropertyReader` and `CatalogUE1OriginalUnrealfilePropertiesReader` both incorrectly consumed 12B regardless of source; `CatalogUE1OriginalUnrealfilePropertySerializationGate::inferUPropertiesByDataBytes` also misclassified May rotation width. Corrected all three and explicitly requires provenance for ambiguous ordinary object headers, while UProperties exact fixed-width data can infer uniquely. Distinct G2 source branch corrected, but full G2/G5 gates remain IN PROGRESS and runtime linking remains unverified.
